# Archive — Historical Radiology Studies

This directory contains historical imaging reports for the department's archived
patient cohort. These records are retained for medicolegal compliance and are
NOT part of the current MDT case review for patient P-2026-0884.
They are provided here as historical context only.

---

## Report 001 — RAD-2015-12-00000

**Study Date**: 2015-12-09
**Patient ID**: P-2019-0117
**Modality**: Chest X-Ray (PA + Lateral)
**Report ID**: RAD-2015-12-00000
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Fever and productive cough, rule out pneumonia vs. cavitating lesion.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  A 12 mm enhancing soft-tissue nodule is identified in the left lower lobe at the level of segment LLL-6. The lesion demonstrates mild internal heterogeneity. No calcification. No satellite lesions. Mediastinal lymph nodes are borderline in size (short-axis 9 mm at 4R station). Recommend PET-CT for metabolic assessment.

**Impression**:
  Stable compared with prior study. Findings remain non-specific.

Electronically signed by Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR
Date: 2015-12-09
Report verified: 2015-12-10

---

## Report 002 — RAD-2016-10-00001

**Study Date**: 2016-10-14
**Patient ID**: P-2024-0139
**Modality**: CT Thorax
**Report ID**: RAD-2016-10-00001
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Incidental finding on CXR, further characterisation required.

**Technique**: Erect inspiratory PA and lateral CXR. No prior available for comparison.

**Findings**:
  No acute intrathoracic abnormality. Lungs are clear bilaterally. No focal consolidation, effusion, or pneumothorax identified. Mediastinum is central. Cardiac silhouette is within normal limits. No hilar adenopathy. Bony thorax: no fracture or destructive lesion. Incidental: mild degenerative change at C5-C6 level.

**Impression**:
  New finding as described — recommend clinical and radiological follow-up.

Electronically signed by Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR
Date: 2016-10-14
Report verified: 2016-10-15

---

## Report 003 — RAD-2015-09-00002

**Study Date**: 2015-09-07
**Patient ID**: P-2025-0128
**Modality**: Nuclear Medicine V/Q Scan
**Report ID**: RAD-2015-09-00002
**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Dyspnoea on exertion, assess for interstitial lung disease.

**Technique**: Erect inspiratory PA and lateral CXR. No prior available for comparison.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

Electronically signed by Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2015-09-07
Report verified: 2015-09-08

---

## Report 004 — RAD-2015-03-00003

**Study Date**: 2015-03-23
**Patient ID**: P-2021-0122
**Modality**: HRCT Lung Protocol
**Report ID**: RAD-2015-03-00003
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Baseline staging for known oesophageal carcinoma.

**Technique**: Erect inspiratory PA and lateral CXR. No prior available for comparison.

**Findings**:
  Known right lower lobe nodule measures 9 mm on current study, stable compared to prior CT dated 18 months ago (8 mm). Margins remain well-defined. No new lesions. Mediastinum unchanged. Pleural surfaces clear. Bones: no suspicious change. Recommend continued annual CT surveillance.

**Impression**:
  Resolved compared with prior study. No further action on this finding.

Electronically signed by Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR
Date: 2015-03-23
Report verified: 2015-03-24

## Report 006 — RAD-2022-09-00005

**Study Date**: 2022-09-04
**Patient ID**: P-2018-0122
**Modality**: HRCT Lung Protocol
**Report ID**: RAD-2022-09-00005
**Radiologist**: Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Dyspnoea on exertion, assess for interstitial lung disease.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

**Findings**:
  There is a 7 mm ground-glass nodule in the right upper lobe, identified at segment RUL-2. Margins are hazy with surrounding faint ground-glass opacity. No satellite nodules. No mediastinal lymphadenopathy. Pleural surfaces intact. No effusion. This finding requires 3-month interval CT to assess for stability.

**Impression**:
  Indeterminate. PET-CT recommended for further characterisation.

Electronically signed by Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2022-09-04
Report verified: 2022-09-05

---

**Clinical Indication**: Haemoptysis, characterise pulmonary lesion.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

No hilar adenopathy. Bony thorax: no fracture or destructive lesion. Incidental: mild degenerative change at C5-C6 level.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

Electronically signed by Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD
Date: 2024-04-23

## Report 009 — RAD-2025-11-00008

**Study Date**: 2025-11-03
**Patient ID**: P-2021-0116
**Modality**: Ultrasound Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2025-11-00008
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Weight loss, dyspnoea, elevated CEA — staging workup.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  Bilateral lower lobe atelectasis, likely postural in aetiology. The right lower lobe shows mild linear opacification consistent with discoid atelectasis. No convincing consolidation. No pleural effusion. Trachea is central. Cardiac size normal. Soft tissue structures unremarkable.

**Impression**:
  Indeterminate. PET-CT recommended for further characterisation.

Electronically signed by Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR
Date: 2025-11-03
Report verified: 2025-11-04

## Report 011 — RAD-2018-01-00010

**Study Date**: 2018-01-26
**Patient ID**: P-2018-0128
**Modality**: PET-CT Whole Body
**Report ID**: RAD-2018-01-00010
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Breathlessness, assess for cardiac and pulmonary cause.

**Technique**: Supine, free-breathing CT of the thorax with IV contrast (80 mL Iohexol 350). Axial 1.25 mm slices reconstructed at 3 mm intervals. MPR coronal and sagittal generated.

**Findings**:
  Post right upper lobectomy changes. Surgical clips in situ. No evidence of local recurrence. Left lung clear. Mediastinal contours stable post-operatively. No pleural collection. No new nodule. Bilateral hilar lymph nodes within normal limits. Bones: healing rib changes from thoracotomy.

**Impression**:
  Resolved compared with prior study. No further action on this finding.

Electronically signed by Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR
Date: 2018-01-26
Report verified: 2018-01-27

## Report 013 — RAD-2023-09-00012

**Study Date**: 2023-09-09
**Patient ID**: P-2021-0106
**Modality**: Nuclear Medicine V/Q Scan
**Report ID**: RAD-2023-09-00012
**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Pre-operative staging for bronchogenic carcinoma.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Pulmonary embolism identified: bilateral segmental and subsegmental filling defects on CT pulmonary angiogram involving the right lower lobe segmental artery (segment 9 and 10 arteries). No right heart strain on current study. No consolidation. No pleural effusion. Recommend anticoagulation per protocol.

**Impression**:
  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

Electronically signed by Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2023-09-09
Report verified: 2023-09-10

**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Evaluation of pleural thickening — mesothelioma vs. reactive.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  There is a 7 mm ground-glass nodule in the right upper lobe, identified at segment RUL-2. Margins are hazy with surrounding faint ground-glass opacity. No satellite nodules. No mediastinal lymphadenopathy. Pleural surfaces intact. No effusion. This finding requires 3-month interval CT to assess for stability.

**Impression**:
  No significant interval change. Continue surveillance as scheduled.

Electronically signed by Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR
Date: 2018-02-15

**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

## Report 035 — RAD-2017-01-00034

**Study Date**: 2017-01-17
**Patient ID**: P-2024-0131
**Modality**: CT Abdomen and Pelvis
**Report ID**: RAD-2017-01-00034
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Fever and productive cough, rule out pneumonia vs. cavitating lesion.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Bilateral lower lobe atelectasis, likely postural in aetiology. The right lower lobe shows mild linear opacification consistent with discoid atelectasis. No convincing consolidation. No pleural effusion. Trachea is central. Cardiac size normal. Soft tissue structures unremarkable.

**Impression**:
  New finding as described — recommend clinical and radiological follow-up.

**Report ID**: RAD-2021-02-00035
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Haemoptysis, characterise pulmonary lesion.

**Technique**: Supine, free-breathing CT of the thorax with IV contrast (80 mL Iohexol 350). Axial 1.25 mm slices reconstructed at 3 mm intervals. MPR coronal and sagittal generated.

**Impression**:
  Pleural disease identified — refer to respiratory team for management.

## Report 039 — RAD-2024-10-00038

**Study Date**: 2024-10-04
**Patient ID**: P-2023-0134
**Modality**: CT Abdomen and Pelvis
**Report ID**: RAD-2024-10-00038
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Weight loss, dyspnoea, elevated CEA — staging workup.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

Electronically signed by Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD
Date: 2024-10-04
Report verified: 2024-10-05

  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  No significant interval change. Continue surveillance as scheduled.

## Report 070 — RAD-2016-03-00069

**Study Date**: 2016-03-05
**Patient ID**: P-2024-0136
**Modality**: Chest X-Ray (PA + Lateral)
**Report ID**: RAD-2016-03-00069
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Haemoptysis, characterise pulmonary lesion.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  Post right upper lobectomy changes. Surgical clips in situ. No evidence of local recurrence. Left lung clear. Mediastinal contours stable post-operatively. No pleural collection. No new nodule. Bilateral hilar lymph nodes within normal limits. Bones: healing rib changes from thoracotomy.

**Impression**:
  Stable compared with prior study. Findings remain non-specific.

Date: 2016-03-05
Report verified: 2016-03-06

## Report 090 — RAD-2018-12-00089

**Study Date**: 2018-12-14
**Patient ID**: P-2019-0103
**Modality**: CT Pulmonary Angiography
**Report ID**: RAD-2018-12-00089
**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Fever and productive cough, rule out pneumonia vs. cavitating lesion.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

Electronically signed by Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2018-12-14
Report verified: 2018-12-15

**Report ID**: RAD-2022-01-00090
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Post-chemotherapy response assessment.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

## Report 101 — RAD-2019-10-00100

**Study Date**: 2019-10-14
**Patient ID**: P-2023-0126
**Modality**: MRI Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2019-10-00100
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Surveillance CT following prior right lower lobe granuloma.

**Technique**: Supine, free-breathing CT of the thorax with IV contrast (80 mL Iohexol 350). Axial 1.25 mm slices reconstructed at 3 mm intervals. MPR coronal and sagittal generated.

**Findings**:
  Pulmonary embolism identified: bilateral segmental and subsegmental filling defects on CT pulmonary angiogram involving the right lower lobe segmental artery (segment 9 and 10 arteries). No right heart strain on current study. No consolidation. No pleural effusion. Recommend anticoagulation per protocol.

**Impression**:
  Pleural disease identified — refer to respiratory team for management.

Electronically signed by Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR
Date: 2019-10-14
Report verified: 2019-10-15

**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

**Impression**:
  Technically limited study due to motion artefact. Recommend repeat.

## Report 127 — RAD-2025-11-00126

**Study Date**: 2025-11-10
**Patient ID**: P-2021-0100
**Modality**: Chest X-Ray (PA + Lateral)
**Report ID**: RAD-2025-11-00126
**Radiologist**: Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Surveillance CT following prior right lower lobe granuloma.

**Technique**: Supine, free-breathing CT of the thorax with IV contrast (80 mL Iohexol 350). Axial 1.25 mm slices reconstructed at 3 mm intervals. MPR coronal and sagittal generated.

**Findings**:
  There is a 7 mm ground-glass nodule in the right upper lobe, identified at segment RUL-2. Margins are hazy with surrounding faint ground-glass opacity. No satellite nodules. No mediastinal lymphadenopathy. Pleural surfaces intact. No effusion. This finding requires 3-month interval CT to assess for stability.

**Impression**:
  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

Electronically signed by Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2025-11-10
Report verified: 2025-11-11

**Patient ID**: P-2019-0111
**Modality**: MRI Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2022-03-00127
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Post-chemotherapy response assessment.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

**Findings**:

  No significant interval change. Continue surveillance as scheduled.

## Report 129 — RAD-2020-07-00128

**Patient ID**: P-2022-0123

**Report ID**: RAD-2020-07-00128
**Radiologist**: Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Baseline staging for known oesophageal carcinoma.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

  Known right lower lobe nodule measures 9 mm on current study, stable compared to prior CT dated 18 months ago (8 mm). Margins remain well-defined. No new lesions. Mediastinum unchanged. Pleural surfaces clear. Bones: no suspicious change. Recommend continued annual CT surveillance.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Baseline staging for known oesophageal carcinoma.

**Technique**: High-resolution CT (HRCT) using expiratory and inspiratory sequences. 1 mm slice thickness.

**Findings**:

## Report 154 — RAD-2023-10-00153

**Study Date**: 2023-10-04
**Patient ID**: P-2022-0125
**Modality**: CT Pulmonary Angiography
**Report ID**: RAD-2023-10-00153
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Surveillance CT following prior right lower lobe granuloma.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

Electronically signed by Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR
Date: 2023-10-04
Report verified: 2023-10-05

## Report 155 — RAD-2019-08-00154

**Study Date**: 2019-08-21
**Patient ID**: P-2024-0126
**Modality**: CT Thorax
**Report ID**: RAD-2019-08-00154
**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Suspected pulmonary embolism — CT-PA indicated.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

**Findings**:
  No acute intrathoracic abnormality. Lungs are clear bilaterally. No focal consolidation, effusion, or pneumothorax identified. Mediastinum is central. Cardiac silhouette is within normal limits. No hilar adenopathy. Bony thorax: no fracture or destructive lesion. Incidental: mild degenerative change at C5-C6 level.

**Impression**:
  Benign appearance. Low probability of malignancy on imaging criteria alone.

Electronically signed by Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2019-08-21
Report verified: 2019-08-22

  No acute intrathoracic abnormality. Lungs are clear bilaterally. No focal consolidation, effusion, or pneumothorax identified. Mediastinum is central. Cardiac silhouette is within normal limits. No hilar adenopathy. Bony thorax: no fracture or destructive lesion. Incidental: mild degenerative change at C5-C6 level.

**Impression**:
  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

Electronically signed by Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2016-07-28
Report verified: 2016-07-28

## Report 179 — RAD-2018-07-00178

**Patient ID**: P-2019-0114
**Modality**: HRCT Lung Protocol
**Report ID**: RAD-2018-07-00178
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Surveillance CT following prior right lower lobe granuloma.

**Technique**: High-resolution CT (HRCT) using expiratory and inspiratory sequences. 1 mm slice thickness.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. **Impression**:
  New finding as described — recommend clinical and radiological follow-up.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

**Report ID**: RAD-2022-02-00183
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Pre-operative staging for bronchogenic carcinoma.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Diffuse ground-glass changes throughout both lungs with a predominant lower lobe distribution. The pattern is consistent with atypical pneumonia / viral aetiology. No lobar consolidation.   Resolved compared with prior study. No further action on this finding.

**Clinical Indication**: Post-chemotherapy response assessment.

**Technique**: Supine, free-breathing CT of the thorax with IV contrast (80 mL Iohexol 350). Axial 1.25 mm slices reconstructed at 3 mm intervals. MPR coronal and sagittal generated.

**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Known pulmonary nodule, interval follow-up.

**Technique**: Supine, free-breathing CT of the thorax with IV contrast (80 mL Iohexol 350). Axial 1.25 mm slices reconstructed at 3 mm intervals. MPR coronal and sagittal generated.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern.   Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Technique**: High-resolution CT (HRCT) using expiratory and inspiratory sequences. 1 mm slice thickness.

  Pleural disease identified — refer to respiratory team for management.

## Report 212 — RAD-2021-08-00211

**Study Date**: 2021-08-02
**Patient ID**: P-2018-0110
**Modality**: Fluoroscopy Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2021-08-00211
**Radiologist**: Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Persistent cough, rule out pulmonary malignancy.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

**Findings**:
  Post right upper lobectomy changes. Surgical clips in situ. No evidence of local recurrence. Left lung clear. Mediastinal contours stable post-operatively. No pleural collection. No new nodule. Bilateral hilar lymph nodes within normal limits. Bones: healing rib changes from thoracotomy.

**Impression**:
  Technically limited study due to motion artefact. Recommend repeat.

Date: 2021-08-02
Report verified: 2021-08-03

**Report ID**: RAD-2016-06-00212
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Weight loss, dyspnoea, elevated CEA — staging workup.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

  There is a 7 mm ground-glass nodule in the right upper lobe, identified at segment RUL-2. Margins are hazy with surrounding faint ground-glass opacity. No satellite nodules.   Pleural disease identified — refer to respiratory team for management.

**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

## Report 268 — RAD-2018-11-00267

**Study Date**: 2018-11-08
**Patient ID**: P-2024-0131
**Modality**: CT Thorax
**Report ID**: RAD-2018-11-00267
**Radiologist**: Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Recurrent chest infections, evaluate for structural abnormality.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

**Findings**:
  There is a 7 mm ground-glass nodule in the right upper lobe, identified at segment RUL-2. Margins are hazy with surrounding faint ground-glass opacity. No satellite nodules. No mediastinal lymphadenopathy. Pleural surfaces intact. No effusion. This finding requires 3-month interval CT to assess for stability.

**Impression**:
  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

Electronically signed by Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR
Date: 2018-11-08

**Modality**: HRCT Lung Protocol
**Report ID**: RAD-2018-01-00276
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Pre-operative staging for bronchogenic carcinoma.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

**Findings**:
  A 12 mm enhancing soft-tissue nodule is identified in the left lower lobe at the level of segment LLL-6. The lesion demonstrates mild internal heterogeneity. No calcification. No satellite lesions. **Impression**:
  Benign appearance. Low probability of malignancy on imaging criteria alone.

## Report 278 — RAD-2024-12-00277

**Study Date**: 2024-12-19
**Patient ID**: P-2021-0134
**Modality**: CT Thorax
**Report ID**: RAD-2024-12-00277
**Radiologist**: Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Persistent cough, rule out pulmonary malignancy.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  Diffuse ground-glass changes throughout both lungs with a predominant lower lobe distribution. The pattern is consistent with atypical pneumonia / viral aetiology. No lobar consolidation. No pleural effusion. Clinical correlation with serology results recommended. Contrast: bilateral hilar prominence, likely reactive.

**Impression**:
  Technically limited study due to motion artefact. Recommend repeat.

Electronically signed by Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR
Date: 2024-12-19
Report verified: 2024-12-20

## Report 279 — RAD-2022-05-00278

**Study Date**: 2022-05-06
**Patient ID**: P-2019-0120
**Modality**: HRCT Lung Protocol
**Report ID**: RAD-2022-05-00278
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Fever and productive cough, rule out pneumonia vs. cavitating lesion.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Resolved compared with prior study. No further action on this finding.

Electronically signed by Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR
Date: 2022-05-06
Report verified: 2022-05-07

## Report 297 — RAD-2018-05-00296

**Study Date**: 2018-05-05
**Patient ID**: P-2022-0104
**Modality**: Fluoroscopy Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2018-05-00296
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Post-chemotherapy response assessment.

**Technique**: High-resolution CT (HRCT) using expiratory and inspiratory sequences. 1 mm slice thickness.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

Electronically signed by Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD
Date: 2018-05-05
Report verified: 2018-05-06

**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

  Technically limited study due to motion artefact. Recommend repeat.

**Report ID**: RAD-2024-10-00325
**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Recurrent chest infections, evaluate for structural abnormality.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

  Resolved compared with prior study. No further action on this finding.

## Report 327 — RAD-2020-09-00326

**Report ID**: RAD-2020-09-00326
**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Haemoptysis, characterise pulmonary lesion.

  Pleural disease identified — refer to respiratory team for management.

## Report 330 — RAD-2016-03-00329

**Study Date**: 2016-03-03
**Patient ID**: P-2021-0118
**Modality**: CT Thorax + Abdomen
**Report ID**: RAD-2016-03-00329
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Incidental finding on CXR, further characterisation required.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Post right upper lobectomy changes. Surgical clips in situ. No evidence of local recurrence. Left lung clear. Mediastinal contours stable post-operatively. No pleural collection. No new nodule. Bilateral hilar lymph nodes within normal limits. Bones: healing rib changes from thoracotomy.

**Impression**:
  Benign appearance. Low probability of malignancy on imaging criteria alone.

Electronically signed by Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR
Date: 2016-03-03
Report verified: 2016-03-04

## Report 331 — RAD-2016-01-00330

**Study Date**: 2016-01-03
**Patient ID**: P-2023-0108
**Modality**: PET-CT Whole Body
**Report ID**: RAD-2016-01-00330
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Baseline staging for known oesophageal carcinoma.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  New finding as described — recommend clinical and radiological follow-up.

Electronically signed by Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR
Date: 2016-01-03
Report verified: 2016-01-04

**Radiologist**: Dr. Li Xue (李雪), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Known pulmonary nodule, interval follow-up.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Diffuse ground-glass changes throughout both lungs with a predominant lower lobe distribution. The pattern is consistent with atypical pneumonia / viral aetiology. No lobar consolidation. ## Report 339 — RAD-2019-07-00338

**Study Date**: 2019-07-12
**Patient ID**: P-2022-0132
**Modality**: CT Abdomen and Pelvis
**Report ID**: RAD-2019-07-00338
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Weight loss, dyspnoea, elevated CEA — staging workup.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Pulmonary embolism identified: bilateral segmental and subsegmental filling defects on CT pulmonary angiogram involving the right lower lobe segmental artery (segment 9 and 10 arteries). No right heart strain on current study. No consolidation. No pleural effusion. Recommend anticoagulation per protocol.

**Impression**:
  Interval progression — suggest MDT discussion.

Date: 2019-07-12

**Radiologist**: Dr. Wang Ying (王颖), MBBS FRANZCR

**Clinical Indication**: Dyspnoea on exertion, assess for interstitial lung disease.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Recurrent chest infections, evaluate for structural abnormality.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

  Pulmonary embolism identified: bilateral segmental and subsegmental filling defects on CT pulmonary angiogram involving the right lower lobe segmental artery (segment 9 and 10 arteries). No right heart strain on current study. No consolidation. No pleural effusion. Recommend anticoagulation per protocol.

**Impression**:
  Benign appearance. Low probability of malignancy on imaging criteria alone.

Electronically signed by Dr. Liu Hong (刘虹), MBBS FRCR
Date: 2025-12-05
Report verified: 2025-12-06

## Report 397 — RAD-2017-08-00396

**Study Date**: 2017-08-12
**Patient ID**: P-2018-0105
**Modality**: Bone Scintigraphy
**Report ID**: RAD-2017-08-00396
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Weight loss, dyspnoea, elevated CEA — staging workup.

**Technique**: Erect inspiratory PA and lateral CXR. No prior available for comparison.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Resolved compared with prior study. No further action on this finding.

Electronically signed by Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD
Date: 2017-08-12
Report verified: 2017-08-13

**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Suspected pulmonary embolism — CT-PA indicated.

**Patient ID**: P-2025-0109
**Modality**: MRI Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2023-06-00425
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Known pulmonary nodule, interval follow-up.

**Technique**: FDG PET-CT: 18F-FDG 370 MBq IV. SUV threshold 2.5. Whole-body scan from vertex to mid-thigh.

## Report 427 — RAD-2019-01-00426

**Study Date**: 2019-01-26
**Patient ID**: P-2018-0115
**Modality**: CT Pulmonary Angiography
**Report ID**: RAD-2019-01-00426
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Known pulmonary nodule, interval follow-up.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Technically limited study due to motion artefact. Recommend repeat.

## Report 443 — RAD-2024-11-00442

**Study Date**: 2024-11-25
**Patient ID**: P-2024-0105
**Modality**: Fluoroscopy Chest
**Report ID**: RAD-2024-11-00442
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Baseline staging for known oesophageal carcinoma.

**Technique**: High-resolution CT (HRCT) using expiratory and inspiratory sequences. 1 mm slice thickness.

**Findings**:
  A 12 mm enhancing soft-tissue nodule is identified in the left lower lobe at the level of segment LLL-6. The lesion demonstrates mild internal heterogeneity. No calcification. No satellite lesions. Mediastinal lymph nodes are borderline in size (short-axis 9 mm at 4R station). Recommend PET-CT for metabolic assessment.

**Report ID**: RAD-2021-10-00453
**Radiologist**: Dr. Huang Bo (黄波), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Suspected pulmonary embolism — CT-PA indicated.

**Technique**: MRI thorax: T1, T2 fat-sat, STIR, DWI sequences. No gadolinium (eGFR borderline).

**Findings**:
  No acute intrathoracic abnormality. Lungs are clear bilaterally. No focal consolidation, effusion, or pneumothorax identified. Mediastinum is central. Cardiac silhouette is within normal limits.   Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

## Report 473 — RAD-2025-04-00472

**Study Date**: 2025-04-22
**Patient ID**: P-2020-0133
**Modality**: Chest X-Ray (PA + Lateral)
**Report ID**: RAD-2025-04-00472
**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

**Clinical Indication**: Evaluation of pleural thickening — mesothelioma vs. reactive.

**Technique**: CT pulmonary angiography with bolus-tracking technique, 100 mL iohexol 350 IV.

**Findings**:
  Diffuse ground-glass changes throughout both lungs with a predominant lower lobe distribution. The pattern is consistent with atypical pneumonia / viral aetiology. No lobar consolidation. No pleural effusion. Clinical correlation with serology results recommended. Contrast: bilateral hilar prominence, likely reactive.

**Impression**:
  Clinically significant finding as above — urgent radiologist discussion recommended.

**Radiologist**: Dr. Chen Mei (陈梅), MD FRCR

## Report 483 — RAD-2017-12-00482

**Patient ID**: P-2024-0101
**Modality**: Nuclear Medicine V/Q Scan
**Report ID**: RAD-2017-12-00482
**Radiologist**: Dr. Zhang Qiang (张强), MD PhD

**Clinical Indication**: Fever and productive cough, rule out pneumonia vs. cavitating lesion.

**Technique**: Unenhanced CT thorax. 5 mm axial slices. Lung window and mediastinal window reviewed.

**Findings**:
  HRCT demonstrates a reticular pattern in the bilateral lung bases with honeycombing and traction bronchiectasis. Findings are consistent with usual interstitial pneumonia (UIP) pattern. No significant ground-glass opacity. Mediastinal lymphadenopathy absent. Impression: UIP pattern, suggest clinical and serological correlation for IPF vs. secondary causes.

**Impression**:
  Stable compared with prior study. Findings remain non-specific.
